# HyperWorks Desktop Tcl reference — HyperWorks Tcl Query (Help 2022.3)

1,120 pages from the shared HWD reference route. This is a page-type grouping, not proof that every row is a callable API. Open the Altair page for its full contract. [Back to route index](./HWD_TCL_REFERENCE_CATALOG_2022_3.md).

| Official page | Exact Type / Application | Syntax | Purpose | Key inputs / outputs / returns |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [::hwp::GetAnimatorCtrl](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwp_general_getanimatorcontrol.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `::hwp::GetAnimatorCtrl` | This procedure implements minus options. This procedure returns an animator control handle of HyperGraph/ HyperView. If no page ID is requested, then it operates on the current/active page. | **Returns:** Returns an animator handle of a page. |
| [::hwp::GetAxis](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwp_getaxis.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `::hwp::GetAxis` | This procedure implements minus options. This procedure returns an axis handle of HyperGraph. If no page ID is requested, then it operates on the current/active page. If no window ID is given, then it returns an axis handle of the active window. | **Returns:** Returns an axis handle to the belonging type. |
| [::hwp::GetClient](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwp_general_getclient.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `::hwp::GetClient` | This procedure implements minus options. This procedure returns a client handle of HyperGraph/HyperView/MotionView. If no page ID is given, then it operates on the current/active page. If no window ID is given, then it returns the client of the active window. Use GetClient function to check for a particular client type. To do this, use the option "-type". If the check fails, then an error is returned. The option "-byHandle" is an exclusive option (no other minus options are allowed). | **Returns:** Returns a client handle. |
| [::hwp::getClosestNode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwp_getclosestnode.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `::hwp::getClosestNode` | This procedure implements minus options. | **Returns:** The node ID of the closest node to this position. |

| [hwIClientInfo GetImageName](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiclientinfo_getimagename.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIClientInfo_handle GetImageName large` | This command finds the name of the client image being used. The name also contains the size of the image (either 24-bit or 16-bit). | **Inputs:** large This variable gets the image name in a bigger or smaller size, depending on your preferences. The input for the large variable is either 1 or 0 (representing true or false). A "1" (true) indicates the you want the larger image size, while "0" (false) indicates you want the smaller image size. |

It differs from GetDisplayName in that it finds the name of the client, rather than the program that the client uses (or its display name). This name is displayed in the tab next to the Session tab if the GUI. | Not stated |

| [hwIDataFile GetChannelMetaDataLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getchannelmetadatalabel.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetChannelMetaDataLabel datatype, request, component, varname` | This command retrieves the label of an item of metadata associated with a specific channel of a data file. The channel is identified by the datatype, request, and component parameters. These are strings which are specific to the particular file. The varname parameter specifies the item of data to retrieve. The label is often more verbose than the varname. It is not necessarily unique - multiple metadata items can have the same labels, even though they must have unique varnames to identify them. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile GetChannelMetaDataList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getchannelmetadatalist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetChannelMetaDataList datatype, request, component` | This command returns a list of the metadata items associated with a specific channel of a data file. The channel is identified by the datatype, request, and component parameters. These strings are specific to the particular file. The returned list will contain the varnames (unique identifier strings) of the items (if any). | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile GetChannelMetaDataType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getchannelmetadatatype.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetChannelMetaDataType datatype, request, component, varname` | This command retrieves the type of an item of metadata associated with a specific channel of a data file. The channel is identified by the datatype, request, and component parameters. These strings are specific to the particular file. The varname parameter specifies which item of data to retrieve. The type will be one of the following: Integer, Float, or String. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile GetChannelMetaDataValue](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getchannelmetadatavalue.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetChannelMetaDataValue datatype, request, component, varname` | This command retrieves an item of metadata associated with a specific channel of a data file. The channel is identified by the datatype, request, and component parameters. These strings are specific to the particular file. The varname parameter specifies which item of data to retrieve. | Not stated |

A channel is a particular combination of a datatype, request, and component. | **Inputs:** datatype The datatype that you choose. request The request that you choose. component The component that you choose. |
| [hwIDataFile GetComponentLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getcomponentlabel.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetComponentLabel` | Returns a string indicating the request’s label. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile GetComponentList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getcomponentlist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetComponentList datatypename,include_time` | Returns a list of requests for the indicated data type is returned. | **Inputs:** datatypename The name of the data type that is being queried. Requests vary by data type. For a list of datatypenames, use data_file_handle GetDataTypeList. include_time(Optional) A filter that determines if Time is to be included as a request. This is not a normal request and defaults to false. |
| [hwIDataFile GetDataTypeList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getdatatypelist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetDataTypeList include_time` | Returns a list of all datatypes. | **Inputs:** include_time (Optional) A filter that determines if Time is to be included as a request. This is not a normal request and defaults to false. |
| [hwIDataFile GetFilename](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getfilename.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetFilename` | Returns a string indicating the filename. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile GetFilteredComponentList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getfilteredcomponentlist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetFilteredComponentList datatype, requestname, include_time` | This command returns a list of components for the specified datatype and request. It differs from GetComponentList in that it filters out components that are marked by the reader as not containing data. Not all readers support this functionality (in fact, most do not). However, there are cases where a file only contains values for some components (X and Y, for example, but not Z) for specific requests. In readers that support this, the reader can specify that a component is empty, in which case it will be filtered out by this command. | **Inputs:** datatype The name of the data type that is being queried. Components vary by data type. For a list of data type names, use data_file_handle GetDataTypeList. requestname The name of the request to be used as a filter. Components vary by request. For a list of request names, use data_file_handle GetRequestList. This command is useful to construct lists in the GUI where you want to provide the user with a list of useful choices based on past selections (datatype and request). However, it is only useful in cases where a specific request has already been selected. Otherwise, GetComponentList must be used. include_time (Optional) A filter that deteremines if Time is to be included as a component. This is not a normal component and defaults to false. |
| [hwIDataFile GetHashValue](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_gethashvalue.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetHashValue` | This command returns the hash value of the file (based on its structure). The command finds the checksum of the structure of the file, making the hash value, which allows you to compare to other data files hash values. You can then check if the two structures are the same. | Not stated |

| [hwIDataFile GetRequestList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getrequestlist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetRequestList datatypename include_time` | Returns a list of requests for the indicated data type is returned. | **Inputs:** datatypename The name of the data type that is being queried. Requests vary by data type. For a list of datatypenames, use data_file_handle GetDataTypeList. include_time (Optional) A filter that determines if Time is to be included as a request. This is not a normal request and defaults to false. |
| [hwIDataFile GetSubcase](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getsubcase.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetSubcase` | Returns the name of the current subcase. hwIDataFile GetDataTypeList returns the list of data types for the current subcase only. | **Inputs:** datatypename The name of the data type that is being queried. Requests vary by data type. For a list of datatypenames, use data_file_handle GetDataTypeList. include_time (Optional) A filter that determines if Time is to be included as a request. This is not a normal request and defaults to false. |
| [hwIDataFile GetSubcaseIDList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getsubcaseidlist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetSubcaseIDList` | Files that do not support subcases will return success, but the list argument will be empty. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile GetSubcaseList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_getsubcaselist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetSubcaseList` | Files that do not support subcases will return Success, but the list argument will be empty. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile GetTimeChannelComponent](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_gettimechannelcomponent.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetTimeChannelComponent datatype, request, component` | This command returns the component name of the time channel associated with a specified data channel. The data channel is specified by the datatype, request, and component parameters. Most data channels have a time channel associated with them. In the majority of cases, all the data channels in a file will be associated with a single, global time channel, but some file formats also support individual data channels having their own associated time channel. The latter case is useful if the file contains data sampled at different rates or from runs of varying length. The term “time” is used loosely (for legacy reasons) to encompass any independent variable that might be associated with a channel. Hence, the actual contents of the time channel might be frequency or RPM in some cases. This command can be used in association with GetTimeChannelDataType and GetTimeChannelRequest to provide the information necessary to plot a data channel against the intended independent variable. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile GetTimeChannelDataType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_gettimechanneldatatype.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetTimeChannelDataType datatype, request, component` | This command returns the datatype name of the time channel associated with a specified data channel. The data channel is specified by the datatype, request, and component parameters. Most data channels have a time channel associated with them. In the majority of cases, all the data channels in a file will be associated with a single, global time channel, but some file formats also support individual data channels having their own associated time channel. The latter case is useful if the file contains data sampled at different rates or from runs of varying length. The term “time” here is used loosely (for legacy reasons) to encompass any independent variable that might be associated with a channel. Hence, the actual contents of the time channel might be frequency or RPM in some cases. This command can be used in association with GetTimeChannelRequest and GetTimeChannelComponent to provide the information necessary to plot a data channel against the intended independent variable. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile GetTimeChannelRequest](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_gettimechannelrequest.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle GetTimeChannelRequest datatype, request, component` | This command returns the request name of the time channel associated with a specified data channel. The data channel is specified by the datatype, request, and component parameters. Most data channels have a time channel associated with them. In the majority of cases, all the data channels in a file will be associated with a single, global time channel, but some file formats also support individual data channels having their own associated time channel. The latter case is useful if the file contains data sampled at different rates or from runs of varying length. The term “time” here is used loosely (for legacy reasons) to encompass any independent variable that might be associated with a channel. Hence, the actual contents of the time channel might be frequency or RPM in some cases. This command can be used in association with GetTimeChannelDataType and GetTimeChannelRequest to provide the information necessary to plot a data channel against the intended independent variable. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile IsChannelMarked](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_ischannelmarked.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle IsChannelMarked datatype, request, component` | This command verifies if the given data channel is marked, and returns true if it is. The channel is identified by the datatype, request, and component parameters. These strings are specific to the particular file. If the given data channel is not marked, it returns false. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile IsFilePresent](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_isfilepresent.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle IsFilePresent` | This command returns whether the data file handle holds a file that exists. It is typically used after a false value is returned while calling the IsValid command. If true is returned, the file is not valid because it is not a data file. If false is returned, the file is not valid because no such file exists. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile IsValid](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_isvalid.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle IsValid` | This command returns whether the data file handle holds a valid file. If the file is valid, a true is returned. If the file is not valid, false is returned. To find why a file is not valid, use the IsFilePresent command. | Not stated |
| [hwIDataFile ReadMarkedChannels](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwidatafile_readmarkedchannels.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIDataFile_handle ReadMarkedChannel datatype, request, component` | This command reads all the marked data channels, returning 0 = HW_Success if successful. The channel is identified by the datatype, request, and component parameters. These strings are specific to the particular file. | Not stated |
| [hwIFont GetFamily](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwifont_getfamily.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIFont_handle GetFamily` | Returns the font family name. | Not stated |
| [hwIFont GetSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwifont_getsize.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIFont_handle GetSize` | Returns the font size. | Not stated |
| [hwIFont GetSlant](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwifont_getslant.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIFont_handle GetSlant` | Returns the font slant, either italic or regular. | Not stated |
| [hwIFont GetWeight](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwifont_getweight.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIFont_handle GetWeight` | Returns the font weight, either bold or regular. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetCompressionLevel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getcompressionlevel.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetCompressionLevel` | Returns a number between 0 and 9. A value of 0 indicates no compression. A value of 9 indicates maximum compression. This command covers only non-lossy compression. The default value for the level is 7, which gives a good balance between speed and size-reduction. The better the compression, the slower the file may be to write. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetCompressOutput](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getcompressoutput.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetCompressOutput` | Returns true or false, depending on whether writing results is enabled. Compression is comprised of two processes: lossy and non-lossy. To produce lossless compression, turn compression on and set the allowable error to zero. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetMaxCompressionError](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getmaxcompressionerror.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetMaxCompressionError` | Returns a number between zero and one, representing the ratio between the allowable absolute error and the maximum difference between values in the set. Compression is comprised of two processes: lossy and non-lossy. To produce lossless compression, turn compression on and set the allowable error to zero. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetWriteAnimation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getwriteanimation.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetWriteAnimation` | Returns true or false, depending on whether compression is enabled. If animation data is not written out with the file, the output can only be used as a model file. However, the file will be much smaller in most cases without the additional data. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetWriteAttributes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getwriteattributes.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetWriteAttributes` | Returns true or false, depending on whether writing attributes is enabled. Graphical entities, such as components, can have attributes, including color or polygon style (shaded versus wireframe). If attributes are not written, default attributes will be applied to the file when it is loaded. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetWriteDisplayedComponents](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getwritedisplayedcomponents.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetWriteDisplayedComponents` | Returns true or false, depending on whether writing displayed components only is enabled. Sometimes, only part of a model is of interest. The remainder of the model can then be rendered invisible by turing off components. In some cases, it is desireable to write out only the remaining portion of the model and the related results. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetWriteEntityIDs](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getwriteentityids.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetWriteEntityIDs` | Returns true or false, depending on whether writing entity IDs is enabled. Largely ignored in 6.0 and later. In previous versions, there was an advantage in efficiency to writing index-based files. This is no longer true. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetWriteHTML](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getwritehtml.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetWriteHTML` | Returns true or false, depending on whether writing an associated HTML file is enabled. If the HyperView Player Browser plug-in has been installed, HyperView can generate an HTML file with the H3D output that will display the H3D file on a web page on any machine. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetWritePreviewImage](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getwritepreviewimage.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetWritePreviewImage` | Returns true or false, depending on whether embedding a JPEG preview is enabled. The H3D format supports holding a JPEG preview image that HyperView Player can display in a browser before the player is activated. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetWriteResults](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getwriteresults.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetWriteResults` | Returns true or false, depending on whether writing results is enabled. By default, any contour, tensor, or vector data displayed in HyperView is written out. Disabling this behavior results in a smaller file. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions GetWriteSolidsAsFaces](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_getwritesolidsasfaces.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle GetWriteSolidsAsFaces` | Returns true or false, depending on whether faces are written. If enabled this option write out wetted faces as shells, saving space over writing the complete set of solid elements. However, only nodal contour data can be displayed on the resulting faces. | Not stated |
| [hwIH3DExportOptions SetCompressOutput](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwih3dexportoptions_setcompressoutput.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIH3DExportOptions_handle SetCompressOutput flag` | Compression is comprised by two processes: lossy and non-lossy. To produce lossless compression, turn compression on and set the allowable error to zero. This command is related to the H3D option on the Publish Session dialog. | **Inputs:** flag True or false. |
| [hwIJPGExportOptions GetQuality](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwijpgexportoptions_getquality.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIJPGExportOptions_handle GetQuality` | Returns the quality used when exporting JPG files. This is a floating point value between 0 and 100 (exclusive). A large quality means better image fidelity but larger image size. This parameter is used when exporting JPG files from any client during HTML export. | Not stated |

| [hwIReportTemplate GetParameterEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwireporttemplate_getparameterenabled.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIReportTemplate GetParameterEnabled name` | This command gets the information whether the given parameter is enabled or not. | **Inputs:** name The name of the parameter. |
| [hwIReportTemplate GetParameterInfo](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwireporttemplate_getparameterinfo.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIReportTemplate_handle GetParameterInfo param_idx` | This command returns a list of information about the input parameter (within {}). The name for the parameter is first on the list, followed by the parameter title in the GUI (within another set of {}). Next in the list is the value of the the parameter input in the GUI (also within {}). Following that is the type in the parameter input (either file or value). If the type of input is a file, the last item on the list will be the type of file. An example of a returned list is: {dat_file {Wheel datafile name} {C:/demos/mv_hv_hg/reports/text_summary/sdf.dat} file *.dat} | **Inputs:** param_idx The index (position) of the parameter that you want to get information for (within the list of all parameters in the report). |
| [hwIReportTemplate GetResolutionsList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwireporttemplate_getresolutionslist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIReportTemplate GetResolutionsList list` | This command gets the resolved TRC (type-request-component) list. | **Inputs:** list The list contains the resolved TRC (type-request-component) data. |
| [hwIReportTemplate GetUnresolvedsList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwireporttemplate_getunresolvedslist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIReportTemplate GetUnresolvedsList list, include_subcase = false, include_resolved = false` | This command gets the unresolved TRC (type-request-component) list for the given report template. | **Inputs:** list The unresolved TRC (type-request-component) list. include_subcase The default value to this would be false. True To include the subcase details in the list. False To not include the subcase details in the list. include_resolved The default value to this would be false. True To include the resolved TRC (type-request-component) in the list. False To not include the resolved TRC (type-request-component) in the list. |
| [hwIReportTemplate LoadResolutions](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwireporttemplate_loadresolutions.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIReportTemplate LoadResolutions filename` | Loads the resolution file. | **Inputs:** filename The filepath of the desired file. |

This is a comma-separated string. to_string The TRC string to which it should to be resolved. This is a comma-separated string. |

| [hwIReportTemplate SetParameterEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwireportemplate_setparameterenabled.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIReportTemplate SetParameterEnabled name, state` | Sets the parameter state to true or false. | **Inputs:** name The name of the given parameter. state True The parameter is enabled. False The parameter is not enabled. |
| [hwISession ApplyReport](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_applyreport.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle ApplyReport report_name, mode, autofit, report_colors, num_params, params` | Applies a report. | **Inputs:** report_name The name of the report (for example, "rep_def_1"). mode overlay Overlay the report onto the current session. append Append the report to the current session. replace Replace the current report. autofit true Autofit plots. false Do not autofit plots. report_colors true Use report colors. false Do not use report colors. num_params The number of parameters. params Additional argument for each *Parameter() statement in the report (report_name). |
| [hwISession CaptureActiveWindow](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_captureactivewindow.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle CaptureActiveWindow type, filename, dimension_type, width, height` | Capture the active window to an image file of a specific dimension. | **Inputs:** type The image file type. Options include: BMP and JPEG. filename The filename that you are writing the captured image to. dimension_type Specifies the width and height units of the final image, which can be in percent or pixels. width The number of width units of the final captured image. height The number of height units of the final captured image. |
| [hwISession CaptureAnimation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_captureanimation.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle CaptureAnimation filetype, filename, [dimension_type, width, height]` | This command uses the current values for options found in the hwIAVIExportOptions or hwIAMFExportOptions object. | **Inputs:** filetype Indicates the format of the output file. The animated formats can be "avi", "amf", and "gif" (one file contains the entire animation) or "png", "jpeg", "bmp" and "tiff" (a numbered series of still image files comprising the entire animation). filename The name of the file to be created. dimension_type Specifies the width and height units of the final animation, which can be in percent or pixels.Note: If you specify a dimension_type, you MUST specify BOTH width and height (see below). width The number of width units of the final captured animation. height The number of height units of the final captured animation. |
| [hwISession CaptureAnimationByArea](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_captureanimationbyarea.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle CaptureAnimationByArea filetype, filename, left, top, right, bottom, [dimension_type, width, height]` | This command uses the current values for options found in the hwIAVIExportOptions or hwIAMFExportOptions object. | **Inputs:** filetype Indicates the format of the output file. The animated formats can be "avi", "amf", and "gif" (one file contains the entire animation) or "png", "jpeg", "bmp" and "tiff" (a numbered series of still image files comprising the entire animation). filename The name of the file to be created. left The coordinate of the left side of the capture region, in screen percentage. top The coordinate of the top of the capture region, in screen percentage. right The coordinate of the right side of the capture region, in screen percentage. bottom The coordinate of the bottom of the capture region, in screen percentage. dimension_type Specifies the width and height units of the final animation, which can be in percent or pixels.Note: If you specify a dimension_type, you MUST specify BOTH width and height (see below). width The number of width units of the final captured animation. height The number of height units of the final captured animation. |
| [hwISession CaptureAnimationByWindow](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_captureanimationbywindow.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle CaptureAnimationByWindow win_id, type, filename, left, top, right, bottom, dimension_type, width, height` | Creates a video file of a window on the current page. | **Inputs:** win_id The ID of the window which needs to be captured. type The type of file that you want the captured movie to be saved as. The available file types are: AVI, AMF, and GIF (one file contains the entire animation) or PNG, JPEG, BMP, and TIFF (a numbered series of still image files comprising the entire animation). filename The file name of the captured video. left The coordinate of the left side of the capture region, in screen percentage (a decimal between 0 and 1). top The coordinate of the top of the capture region, in screen percentage (a decimal between 0 and 1). right The coordinate of the right side of the capture region, in screen percentage (a decimal between 0 and 1). bottom The coordinate of the bottom of the capture region, in screen percentage (a decimal between 0 and 1). dimension_type Specifies the width and height units of the final animation, which can be in percent or pixels. width The width of the captured video in percent/pixels. height The height of the captured video in percent/pixels. |
| [hwISession CaptureScreen](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_capturescreen.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle CaptureScreen type, filename, quality` | Captures the current screen to an image file. | **Inputs:** type The type of file. Options include: BMP, JPEG, TIFF, PNG, Clipboard filename The filename of the captured image. quality The level of quality or compression (default is 100). |
| [hwISession CaptureScreenToSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_capturescreentosize.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle CaptureScreenToSize type, filename, height, width, quality` | Captures the current screen to an image file of the specified pixel resolution. | **Inputs:** type The type of file. Options include: BMP, JPEG, TIFF, PNG, Clipboard filename The filename of the captured image. width (Optional) The width of the captured image in pixels (default in batch mode is 1024, otherwise default is value returned by hwISession::GetPageWidth). height (Optional) The height of the captured image in pixels (default in batch mode is 768, otherwise default is value returned by hwISession::GetPageHeight). quality (Optional) The level of quality or compression (default is 100). This is used only in JPEG type captures. |
| [hwISession ClearExternalSolvers](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_clearexternalsolvers.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle ClearExternalSolvers` | Clears the list of registered external solvers. | Not stated |

| [hwISession GetSpaceballButtonMin](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_getspaceballbuttonmin.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle GetSpaceballButtonMin` | Returns the highest Spaceball button ID that can be mapped to a Tcl callback via the RegisterSpaceballCallback procedure. | Not stated |
| [hwISession GetSystemVariable](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_getsystemvariable.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle GetSystemVariable name` | Returns the value associated with the given variable name. | **Inputs:** name The application variable name (key). See below. Below is a list of the available variable names. Variable names are case-sensitive. All returned paths are platform dependent for the current platform. "ALTAIR_HOME" Path to the installation directory. "NAME" Name of the current HyperWorks product. "TITLE Version title of the HyperWorks product. "VERSION Release version (not beta version). "CURRENTWORKINGDIR Gets the current working directory. "EXECUTABLEPATH Full executable path to the current HyperWorks product. "EXECUTABLEDIR Bin directory for the current HyperWorks product. "hw_tcl_common Tcl installation directory installed with HyperWorks. "tcl_lib Tcl libraries directory under the Tcl installation directory installed with HyperWorks. "tk_lib Tk libraries directory under the Tcl installation directory installed with HyperWorks. "hw_readers" Direct readers directory for the animation client. "hm_feinput_readers Import translators directory for HyperMesh and the FE Model client. "hm" Path to the HyperMesh executable. "hm_mac" Path to the HyperMesh root macro file. "TEMPLATES_DIR" Path to the templates directory. "hm_user_profiles" Path to the HyperMesh UserProfiles directory. "HSTBIN_DIR" Path to the HyperStudy bin directory. "study_proxy_path" Path to the HyperStudy bin directory. "PREFERENCES_HYPERSTUDY" Full path to the current preferences file used by HyperStudy. "DOE_DESIGNS_DIR" Path to the HyperStudy doe_designs directory. "mv_solver_writers" Path to the MotionView bin directory. "hw_old_readers" Path to the emi directory under the externalreaders directory, which contains the readers for the animation client in HyperWorks 5.1 "altair_lic.dat" Path to the license file. "hm_dll_readers" Path to the bin directory under HyperMesh. "hm_scripts_dir" Path to the scripts directory under HyperMesh. "hyperbeam_dir" Path to HyperBeam. "wish" Path to the Tcl wish executable. "EXTERNALREADERS_DIR" Path to the directory containing the external readers for the plot client. "EXTERNALWRITERS_DIR" Path to the directory containing the external writers for the plot client. "EXTERNALFUNCS_DIR" Path to the directory containing the external math functions for the plot client. "export_templates_dir" Path to the directory containing the curve export templates for the plot client. "standard_statistics_template" Path to the stats.tpl template file "hw_help_dir" Path to the help directory under the HyperWorks installation. "nastran_to_abaqus_converter" Directory containing the Nastran to Abaqus converter. "jre_path" Directory containing the JAVA installation under the HyperWorks installation. "epic_path" Epic root directory. "hws_path" Path to the libraries under the hw directory. "ARCHITECTURE" Returns the operating system name (for Unix only). "XDISPLAYNAME" Returns the display name (for Unix only). |

This command uses the current values for the hwIAVIExportOptions, hwIH3DexportOptions, and hwIJPGExportOptions objects in the current session. To manipulate these values, obtain a handle to the desired object and set the desired property before executing this command. | **Inputs:** filename The filename of the HTML or XML report. frame_width The width (as a percentage of the screen) of the browser frame. Values can be from 10 to 40 (inclusive). |
| [hwISession RegisterExternalFunction](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_registerexternalfunction.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle RegisterExternalFunction filename, function_name, num_arguments` | Registers the given external function. | **Inputs:** filename The name of the dll or shared object file to be registered. function_name The name of the function. num_arguments The number of arguments. |
| [hwISession RegisterExternalReader](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_registerexternalreader.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle RegisterExternalReader filename, type, ext, category` | Registers the given external reader. | **Inputs:** filename The name of the reader to be registered. type (Optional) The type of the reader to be registered. This string, if specified, appears in the drop-down combo box of the File dialog. ext (Optional) If specified, this is the extension actually used as a filter if the specified type is chosen in the File dialog. category (Optional) The category of the reader to be registered. Options include “asc” (ASCII), “bin” (binary), or left unspecified for a generic category (which is the default). |
| [hwISession RegisterExternalSolver](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_registerexternalsolver.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle RegisterExternalSolver name, path` | Appends an external solver to the list of registered external solvers. | **Inputs:** name The name of the external solver. path The path to the external solver executable. |
| [hwISession RegisterImportTemplate](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_registerimporttemplate.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle RegisterImportTemplate filename, type, ext` | Registers the given import template. | **Inputs:** filename The name of the template to be registered. type (Optional) The type of template to be registered. This string, if specified, appears in the drop-down combo box in the File dialog. ext (Optional) If specified, this is the extension actually used as a filter if the specified type is chosen in the File dialog. Also, the registered import template will be among the first tried when files with this extension are imported (accelerating the file recognition process). |
| [hwISession RegisterSpaceballCallback](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_registerspaceballcallback.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle RegisterSpaceballCallback buttonId, procName` | Allows registration of Tcl callbacks for Spaceball button events. | **Inputs:** buttonID The button ID to register a callback against. Valid values are identified by calling the GetSpaceballButtonMin and GetSpaceballButtonMax commands. procName The name of the procedure to call when the Spaceball event occurs. The procedure takes a state parameter, which is the state of the button at the time that it is pressed. True Indicates the button is pressed. False Indicates the button is released. If the procedure is not in the Tcl global namespace, the namespace must be specified. |
| [hwISession RegisterTemplexFunction](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_registertemplexfunction.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle RegisterTemplexFunction name, function` | Registers the given Templex function. | **Inputs:** name The name of the function to be registered. function The body of the function to be registered. |
| [hwISession SetExportMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_setexportmode.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle SetExportMode mode` | Sets the export mode for the page. | **Inputs:** mode "html" sets the mode to HTML. This function, along with hwIPage SetIsExporting, replaces the command hwIPage SetExportToHTML. |
| [hwISession UnloadXMLFile](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwisession_unloadxmlfile.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwISession_handle UnloadXMLFile filename` | Unloads a previously loaded XML file, freeing the associated memory. | **Inputs:** filename The name of a previously loaded file. |
| [hwITemplex Evaluate](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwitemplex_evaluate.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwITemplex_handle Evaluate myexpression, type` | Returns a string indicating the evaluated output of myexpression. | **Inputs:** myexpression The expression to be evaluated. type If set to ‘true’, ‘template’, or ‘templex’, the expression is evaluated using the Templex language. Otherwise, it is evaluated as a simple math expression. |
| [hwITemplex GetMathFunctionsList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwitemplex_getmathfunctionslist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwITemplex_handle GetMathFunctionsList` | Returns a list of all math functions available to this hwITemplex object. | Not stated |
| [hwITemplex GetNumberOfFunctionParameters](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwitemplex_getnumberoffunctionparameters.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwITemplex_handle GetNumberOfFunctionParameters funcname` | Determines the number of required parameters for a specified math function. | **Inputs:** funcname The function for which the number of required parameters is desired. Returns the number of required parameters for the given function. -1 denotes an error. |
| [hwIViewControl CircleZoom](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiviewcontrol_circlezoom.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIViewControl_handle CircleZoom min_x, min_y, max_x, max_y` | This command zooms on the graph based on the given dimensions. It differs from ZoomIn or ZoomOut in that it zooms within the specified dimensions rather than a preset zoom. It also differs from Fit in that it zooms based on the user-input dimensions rather than zooming to the curve’s dimensions. | **Inputs:** min_x The minimum value of x. This is the lowest possible value of x on the graph (the most left value). This does not mean that the x axis goes down to this exact x value. It does, however, mean that the displayed portion of the x axis will not stop before this value. max_x The maximum value of x. This is the highest possible value of x on the graph (the most right value). This does not mean that the x axis goes up to this exact x value. It does, however, mean that the displayed portion of the x axis will not stop before this value. min_y The minimum value of y. This is the lowest value of y on the graph (the bottom most value). This does not mean that the y value goes down to this exact y value. It does, however, mean that the displayed portion of the y axis will not stop before this value. max_y The maximum value of y. This is the highest value of y on the graph (the upper most value). This does not mean that the y axis goes up to this exact y value. It does, however, mean that the displayed portion of the y axis will not stop before this value. |
| [hwIWindow Draw](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiwindow_draw.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIWindow_handle Draw` | Draws the client object. | Not stated |

Once set, the ratios can be frozen by calling SetAspectRatioLocked. If not locked, the ratios may be changed by manipulations of the GUI. | **Inputs:** locked Keep axes fixed, true or false. |
| [plt3DIPlot SetAxesScaleMethod](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/plt3diplot_setaxesscalemethod.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `plt3DIPlot_handle SetAxesScaleMethod method` | The aspect ratios of the axes can be set explicitly. The X axis is assumed always to have a length of one, with Y and Z axes having individual values associated with them to define the ratios. Once set, the ratios can be frozen by calling SetAspectRatioLocked. If not locked, the ratios may be changed by manipulations of the GUI. | **Inputs:** method byviewport Stretches the axes to maximize the window area. byrange Displays units as the same length along each axis. none Displays the axes as a cubical box. |
| [plt3DIPlot SetAxesType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/plt3diplot_setaxestype.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `plt3DIPlot_handle SetAxesType type` | Sets whether the axes are displayed. | **Inputs:** type The axes type. |
| [plt3DIPlot SetBackgroundColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/plt3diplot_setbackgroundcolor.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `plt3DIPlot_handle SetBackgroundColor color` | Sets the current background color of the HyperGraph 3D window. | **Inputs:** color Three values representing red, green, and blue. color can also be the color name or a single value from 0 - 63 representing an index on the HyperWorks color wheel. |
| [plt3DIPlot SetClipTransparency](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/plt3diplot_setcliptransparency.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `plt3DIPlot_handle SetClipTransparency enabled` | Data, in the form of waterfalls, curves, or surfaces, can be clipped with planes. The undisplayed portion of the data can be rendered partially visibible by means of this flag. | **Inputs:** enabled Display clipped data as transparent, true or false. |
| [plt3DIPlot SetContourEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/plt3diplot_setcontourenabled.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `plt3DIPlot_handle SetContourEnabled state` | The contour is based on the W vector. | **Inputs:** state True Turns on the contour. False Turns off the contour. |
| [plt3DIPlot SetCurvePicking](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/plt3diplot_setcurvepicking.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `plt3DIPlot_handle SetCurvePicking enabled` | The default behavior when a waterfall is picked with the mouse is to report the varname of the waterfall. If you want to know which slice (curve) was picked, you must enable curve picking (a similar principle applies with curve groups). | **Inputs:** enabled Enabled, true or false. |
| [plt3DIPlot SetDiscreteColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/plt3diplot_setdiscretecolor.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `plt3DIPlot_handle SetDiscreteColor state` | Displays the contour lines with edges or blended. | **Inputs:** state True Turns on discrete contour shading. False Turns on blended contour shading. |
| [plt3DIPlot SetForegroundColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/plt3diplot_setforegroundcolor.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `plt3DIPlot_handle SetForegroundColor color` | Sets the current foreground color of the HyperGraph window. | **Inputs:** color Three values representing red, green, and blue. color can also be the color name or a single value from 0 - 63 representing an index on the HyperWorks color wheel. |
| [plt3DIPlot SetGridLineColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/plt3diplot_setgridlinecolor.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `plt3DIPlot_handle SetGridLineColor color` | Sets the current axes grid line color of the HyperGraph 3D window. | **Inputs:** color Three values representing red, green, and blue. color can also be the color name or a single value from 0 - 63 representing an index on the HyperWorks color wheel. |
| [plt3DIPlot SetIncludeAxesInFit](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/plt3diplot_setincludedaxesinfit.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `plt3DIPlot_handle SetIncludeAxesInFit enabled` | In box axis mode, by default, the axes box is included in bounding box calculations, so as to keep it visibile when a fit operation occurs. Turning this flag off causes the plot to appear larger after a fit operation, but parts of the axes box may not be visible. | **Inputs:** enabled Keep axes visible, true or false. |

The coordinates must begin with the coordinate axis (“x”, “y”, or “z”), followed by the coordinate number (“0123456789.+-“) with a space between the two values. The list must be within “”. | [pltIPlot Draw](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/pltiplot_draw.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `pltIPlot_handle Draw` | Changes made by other methods may not be displayed properly until Draw is called. A common practice is to make multiple changes and then call Draw only once, limiting the number of redraws. | Not stated |
| [pltIPlot ExportEMF](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/pltiplot_exportemf.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `pltIPlot_handle ExportEMF filename width height` | Exports the plot as an EMF file. | **Inputs:** filename Enter a filename, for example, ExportEMF test.emf, to export your plot with the same screen resolution as displayed in the GUI. filename width Enter a new filename, as well as the desired width. The plot is output and rendered the first time using the width specified, and the ratio is calculated to display the correct height so the plot stays proportionate. For example, ExportEMF test_3840.emf 3840 outputs the plot with the width of 3840. filename width height Enter a new filename, as well as the desired width and height. The plot is output and rendered the first time using the width and height specified. If you enter both the width and height, then the values are hard-coded into the file. For example, ExportEMF test_3840_2048.emf 3840 2048 outputs the plot with the width of 3840 and a height of 2048. |

| [pltIViewControl CircleZoom](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/pltiviewcontrol_circlezoom.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `hwIViewControl_handle CircleZoom min_x, min_y, max_x, max_y` | This command zooms on the graph based on the given dimensions. It differs from ZoomIn or ZoomOut in that it zooms within the specified dimensions given by the user rather than a preset zoom. It also differs from Fit in that it zooms based on user-given dimensions rather than zooming to the curve’s dimensions. | **Inputs:** min_x The minimum value of x. This is the lowest value of x on the graph (the left-most value). This does not mean that the x axis will go down to this exact x value. It does, however, mean that the shown portion of the x-axis will not stop before this value. max_x The maximum value of x. This is the highest value of x on the graph (the right-most value). This does not mean that the x-axis will go up to this exact x value. It does, however, mean that the shown portion of the x-axis will not stop before this value. min_y The minimum value of y. This is the lowest value of y on the graph (the bottom-most value). This does not mean that the y value will go down to this exact y value. It does, however, mean that the shown portion of the y-axis will not stop before this value. max_y The maximum value of y. This is the highest value of y on the graph (the upper-most value). This does not mean that the y-axis will go up to this exact y value. It does, however, mean that the shown portion of the y-axis will not stop before this value. |

| [pulIMacro GetNoteText](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/pulimacro_getnotetext.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `pulIMacro GetNoteText` | This command returns the text of the current macro’s note. | Not stated |

| [tblIRule SetBackgroundColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/tblirule_setbackgroundcolor.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `tblIRule_handle SetBackgroundColor bgColor` | To set up conditional formatting of table cells,you must first add a rule. Next, you must get the rule handle and set the relevant parameters on the rule handle. These parameters are used to format the cells that are part of the rule. | [tblIRule SetCellList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/tblirule_setcelllist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `tblIRule_handle SetCellList cellList` | To set up conditional formatting of table cells, you must first add a rule. One such parameter is the list of cell names on which this rule should act. This API helps in setting the same. | **Inputs:** cellList The list of cell names. For example, A1 A2. |
| [tblIRule SetOperator](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/tblirule_setoperator.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `tblIRule_handle SetOperator compOperator` | To set up conditional formatting of table cells, you must first add a rule. Next, you must get the rule handle and set the relevant parameters on the rule handle. These parameters are used to decide the formatting of the cells that are part of the rule. One such parameter is the comparison operator used to compare the reference value with the actual value in the cell. This API helps in setting the same. | **Inputs:** compOperator The comparison operator used to compare the reference value with the actual value in the cell. Valid alignment values are "=" , ">" , ">=" ,"<" and "<=". |
| [tblIRule SetTextColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/tblirule_settextcolor.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `tblIRule_handle SetTextColor color` | To set up conditional formatting of table cells, you must first add a rule. | [tblIRule SetValue](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/tblirule_setvalue.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `tblIRule_handle SetValue ValueToCompareWith` | To set up conditional formatting of table cells, you must first add a rule. One such parameter is the reference value that is to be compared with the actual value in the cell to determine if the cell passes the rule. The arguments are as follows below. | **Inputs:** marker_handle The name to be assigned to the returned handle. ID The internal ID number of the marker in the video specified by the video_handle. |
| [vidIVideo GetUseTracking](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_getusetracking.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIVideo_handle GetUseTracking` | This command returns a boolean flag specifying whether a coordinate system, if defined, will be used in calculating measure information such as endpoint location and length. | Not stated |
| [vidIVideo GetUseUnits](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_getuseunits.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIVideo_handle GetUseUnits` | Gets a flag which indicates whether the annotations of measures lengths and endpoint coordinates are reported using the user defined unit system and conversion factor, or in pixels. | Not stated |
| [vidIVideo ImportTrackerList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_importtrackerlist.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `video_handle ImportTrackerList filename` | Imports (loads) the markers previously saved to the specified marker file into the video. | **Inputs:** filename The marker filename. Marker files have the extension “mark”. |
| [vidIVideo IsFilenameShown](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_isfilenameshown.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIVideo_handle IsFilenameShown` | This command finds whether the filename is shown on the video or not. If the filename is turned on, true is returned. If not, false is returned. If the filename is turned on, it is displayed within the annotation note. | Not stated |
| [vidIVideo IsFramenumberShown](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_isframenumbershown.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIVideo_handle IsFramenumberShown` | This command finds whether the frame number is shown on the video or not. The frame number shows the specific frame number of the video, which is displayed within the annotation note. If the frame number is shown, true is returned. If not, false is returned. | Not stated |
| [vidIVideo IsTimestepShown](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_istimestepshown.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIVideo_handle IsTimestepShown` | This command finds whether the time step is shown on the video or not. The time step shows the time stamp of the video, which is displayed within the annotation note. If the time step is shown, true is returned. If not, false is returned. | Not stated |
| [vidIVideo IsTolerance](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_istolerance.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIVideo_handle IsTolerance` | This command finds if the tolerance is on. Tolerance is the allotted difference in timing between two videos. If the timing between two videos is greater than the tolerance, the second video (or third, fourth and so on) will not play the frame(s) corresponding with that specific time. Note: For this function to work, at least two videos must be in the page. If the tolerance is on, true is returned. Otherwise, false is returned. | Not stated |
| [vidIVideo MasterMeasureShown](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_mastermeasureshown.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIVideo_handle MasterMeasureShown` | If the flag is true, the master measure is displayed on the video, otherwise it is hidden. | Not stated |
| [vidIVideo RemoveMovie](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_removemovie.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIVideo_handle RemoveMovie index` | Removes a movie. | **Inputs:** index The index of the movie. |
| [vidIVideo RemoveNote](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_removenote.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIVideo_handle RemoveNote idx` | Removes the specified note from the video. | **Inputs:** idx The index of the note to be removed. Must be positive. |
| [vidIVideo SetCSYaxisColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidivideo_setcsyaxiscolor.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIVideo_handle GetCSYaxisColor` | Gets the color used for drawing the Y axis arrow of the user coordinate system symbol. | Not stated |
| [vidIViewControl CircleZoom](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidiviewcontrol_circlezoom.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIViewControl_handle CircleZoom min_x min_y max_x max_y` | Returns the added movie index. | **Inputs:** min_x Minimum x value of the zoom rectangle. min_y Minimum y value of the zoom rectangle. max_x Maximum x value of the zoom rectangle. max_x Maximum y value of the zoom rectangle. |
| [vidIViewControl Fit](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidiviewcontrol_fit.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIViewControl_handle Fit` | Unzooms video or image. | Not stated |
| [vidIViewControl PickCenter](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidiviewcontrol_pickcenter.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIViewControl_handle PickCenter x y` | Centers plot based on the chosen point. | **Inputs:** x X coordinate of the new center. y Y coordinate of the new center. |
| [vidIViewControl Recenter](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidiviewcontrol_recenter.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIViewControl_handle Recenter` | Recenters video or image. | Not stated |
| [vidIViewControl Translate](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/vidiviewcontrol_translate.htm) | HyperWorks Tcl Query | `vidIViewControl_handle Translate x y` | Translates the contents of the current window to the given position. | **Inputs:** x The x position. y The y position. |
